  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/extractors_classes.py\n@@\n def _extract_classes_from_file(filepath: str, lines: list[str]) -> list[ClassInfo]:\n@@\n-        attributes = _extract_init_attributes(lines, class_start, class_end)\n+        attributes = _extract_init_attributes(\n+            lines,\n+            class_start,\n+            class_end,\n+            dataclass_decorated=_has_dataclass_decorator(lines, class_start),\n+        )\n@@\n def _extract_methods(lines: list[str], start: int, end: int) -> list[FunctionInfo]:\n@@\n     return methods\n \n \n def _extract_init_attributes(\n-    lines: list[str], class_start: int, class_end: int\n+    lines: list[str],\n+    class_start: int,\n+    class_end: int,\n+    *,\n+    dataclass_decorated: bool = False,\n ) -> list[str]:\n     \"\"\"Extract self.x = ... attribute names from __init__.\"\"\"\n     attrs = set()\n     in_init = False\n     init_indent = 0\n+    class_indent = len(lines[class_start]) - len(lines[class_start].lstrip())\n+    class_field_re = re.compile(r\"^\\s*(\\w+)\\s*:\\s*[^=].*$\")\n \n     for idx in range(class_start, class_end):\n         stripped = lines[idx].strip()\n@@\n             for attr_match in re.finditer(r\"self\\.(\\w+)\\s*=\", lines[idx]):\n                 attrs.add(attr_match.group(1))\n \n+    if dataclass_decorated:\n+        for idx in range(class_start + 1, class_end):\n+            line = lines[idx]\n+            stripped = line.strip()\n+            if (\n+                not stripped\n+                or stripped.startswith(\"#\")\n+                or stripped.startswith(\"@\")\n+                or stripped.startswith(\"def \")\n+                or stripped.startswith(\"async def \")\n+            ):\n+                continue\n+            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n+            # Count only class-body attribute annotations, not nested blocks.\n+            if indent != class_indent + 4:\n+                continue\n+            match = class_field_re.match(stripped)\n+            if match:\n+                attrs.add(match.group(1))\n+\n     return sorted(attrs)\n+\n+\n+def _has_dataclass_decorator(lines: list[str], class_start: int) -> bool:\n+    \"\"\"Check if the class has @dataclass / @dataclasses.dataclass decorator.\"\"\"\n+    idx = class_start - 1\n+    while idx >= 0:\n+        stripped = lines[idx].strip()\n+        if not stripped:\n+            idx -= 1\n+            continue\n+        if not stripped.startswith(\"@\"):\n+            break\n+        decorator = stripped[1:]\n+        if decorator.startswith(\"dataclass\") or decorator.startswith(\n+            \"dataclasses.dataclass\"\n+        ):\n+            return True\n+        idx -= 1\n+    return False\n*** End Patch\n"
}

'\\n'.join(lines)\n\n\ndef rewrite_facade_imports(text: str, facade: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    block_re = re.compile(\n        rf'(?ms)^([ \\t]*)from {re.escape(facade)} import \\(\\n(.*?)^\\1\\)\\s*\\n?'\n    )\n    line_re = re.compile(\n        rf'(?m)^([ \\t]*)from {re.escape(facade)} import ([^\\n]+)\\n?'\n    )\n\n    def       module = mapping.get(name)\n            if module is None:\n                raise KeyError(f'Unknown symbol {name!r} for facade {facade}')\n            groups.setdefault(module, [])\n            groups[module].append(f\"{name} as {alias}\" if alias else name)\n        changed = True\n        rendered = [format_group(indent, module, syms) for module, syms in groups.items()]\n        return re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        return repl(m.group(1), m.group(2))\n\n    text3 = line_re.sub(line_sub, text2)\n    return text3, changed\n\n\ndef process(facade: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> int:\n    count = 0\n    for path in ROOT.rglob('*.py'):\n        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)\n        if '.venv' in rel.parts or '__pycache__' in rel.parts:\n            continue\n        text = path.read_text()\n        if f'from {facade} import' not in text:\n            continue\n        new_text, changed = rewrite_facade_imports(text, facade, mapping)\n        if changed and new_text != text:\n            path.write_text(new_text)\n            count += 1\n    return count\n\nscoring_count = process('desloppify.scoring', SCORING_MAP)\nwork_queue_count = process('desloppify.engine.work_queue', WORK_QUEUE_MAP)\nprint(f'scoring files rewritten: {scoring_count}')\nprint(f'work_queue files rewritten: {work_queue_count}')\nPY",

'ATTEST_EXAMPLE': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.core',\n    'QueueBuildOptions': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.core',\n    'WorkQueueResult': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.core',\n    'build_work_queue': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.core',\n    'group_queue_items': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.core',\n    'ALL_STATUSES': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'primary_command_for_issue': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'review_issue_weight': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'scope_matches': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'slugify': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'status_matches': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'supported_fixers_for_item': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers',\n    'build_subjective_items': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic',\n    'subjective_strict_scores': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic',\n    'expire_stale_holistic': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.issues',\n    'impact_label': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.issues',\n    'list_open_review_issues': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.issues',\n    'update_investigation': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.issues',\n    'build_issue_items': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking',\n    'item_explain': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking',\n    '[REDACTED],\n    'subjective_score_value': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking',\n    'collapse_clusters': 'desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order',\n}\n\n\ndef parse_items(raw: str) -> list[tuple[str, str | None]]:\n    items: list[tuple[str, str | None]] = []\n    for piece in raw.replace('\\n', ' ').split(','):\n        token = piece.split('#', 1)[0].strip()\n        if not token:\n            continue\n        m = re.match(r'^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)(?:\\s+as\\s+([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))?$', token)\n        if not m:\n            raise ValueError(f'Unsupported import token: {token!r}')\n        items.append((m.group(1), m.group(2)))\n    return items\n\n\ndef format_group(indent: str, module: str, symbols: list[str]) -> str:\n    if len(symbols) == 1:\n        return f\"{indent}from {module} import {symbols[0]}\"\n    lines = [f\"{indent}from {module} import (\"]\n    for sym in symbols:\n        lines.append(f\"{indent}    {sym},\")\n    lines.append(f\"{indent})\")\n    return '\\n'.join(lines)\n\n\ndef rewrite_facade_imports(text: str, facade: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    block_re = re.compile(\n        rf'(?ms)^([ \\t]*)from {re.escape(facade)} import \\(\\n(.*?)^\\1\\)\\n?'\n    )\n    line_re = re.compile(\n        rf'(?m)^([ \\t]*)from {re.escape(facade)} import (?!\\()([^\\n]+)\\n?'\n    )\n\n    def repl(indent: str, body: str) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        items = parse_items(body)\n        groups: OrderedDict[str, list[str]] = OrderedDict()\n        for name, alias in items:\n            module = mapping.get(name)\n            if module is None:\n                raise KeyError(f'Unknown symbol {name!r} for facade {facade}')\n            groups.setdefault(module, [])\n            groups[module].append(f\"{name} as {alias}\" if alias else name)\n        changed = True\n        rendered = [format_group(indent, module, syms) for module, syms in groups.items()]\n        return '\\n'.join(rendered) + '\\n'\n\n    text2 = block_re.sub(lambda m: repl(m.group(1), m.group(2)), text)\n    text3 = line_re.sub(lambda m: repl(m.group(1), m.group(2)), text2)\n    return text3, changed\n\n\ndef process(facade: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> int:\n    count = 0\n    for path in ROOT.rglob('*.py'):\n        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)\n        if '.venv' in rel.parts or '__pycache__' in rel.parts:\n            continue\n        text = path.read_text()\n        if f'from {facade} import' not in text:\n            continue\n        new_text, changed = rewrite_facade_imports(text, facade, mapping)\n        if changed and new_text != text:\n            path.write_text(new_text)\n            count += 1\n    return count\n\nprint('scoring files rewritten:', process('desloppify.scoring', SCORING_MAP))\nprint('work_queue files rewritten:', process('desloppify.engine.work_queue', WORK_QUEUE_MAP))\nPY",

}

> DEVELOPER

So what do they do? Do tehy do anything or do we just need to detatch it?

> AGENT

You’re asking about the D12 items specifically. I’m going to map each one to its current runtime behavior so we can decide whether to detach or keep them without guessing.

> AGENT

They do something today. They are not dead in code, even if your current `plan.json` doesn’t actively use all values.

- `promoted_ids`:
  - Used to preserve manual priority when queue sync injects subjective items.
  - Written in `move top`, consumed when inserting synthetic IDs.
  - See [operations_queue.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_queue.py), [stale_dimensions.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py).

- Override `description` / `note`:
  - Backed by real CLI commands (`plan describe`, `plan note`) and shown in plan status.
  - Also carried during reconcile/supersede flows.
  - See [override_handlers.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py), [cmd.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py), [reconcile.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py).

- Skip kinds `temporary` / `permanent` / `false_positive`:
  - Not cosmetic. `permanent` and `false_positive` route into state-layer status (`wontfix` / `false_positive`) and affect strict scoring.
  - `temporary` supports resurfacing via `review_after`.
  - See [operations_skip.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py), [override_handlers.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py), [schema.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py).

So: I would not “detach” by default.  
If you want to intentionally simplify product behavior, we can remove them, but that is a behavior change (not just cleanup).

> AGENT

They do real work, even though your current `plan.json` happens to have none of them populated.

- `promoted_ids` is a front-of-queue “user priority barrier”.  
  Set when you explicitly reorder to `top` ([operations_queue.py:129](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_queue.py:129)), then used so system-injected subjective/triage items go *after* promoted items ([stale_dimensions.py:174](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:174), [stale_dimensions.py:423](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:423)).

- Override `description`/`note` are user annotations from CLI (`plan describe`, `plan note`) ([override_handlers.py:96](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:96), [override_handlers.py:121](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:121)), stored in overrides ([operations_meta.py:50](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_meta.py:50)), and rendered in plan output ([render_sections.py:46](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/render_sections.py:46)).

- `permanent` / `false_positive` skip kinds are not cosmetic.  
  They drive validation + attestation ([override_handlers.py:158](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:158)), update state status (`wontfix` / `false_positive`) ([override_handlers.py:182](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:182)), and are reopened on unskip ([override_handlers.py:306](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:306), [operations_skip.py:45](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py:45)).

Yes, it can be streamlined while keeping behavior:

1. Centralize skip-kind rules in one policy map (requirements, state status mapping, reopen behavior, labels) and remove duplicated conditionals.  
2. Centralize promoted-id handling (`mark`, `prune`, `insertion_index`) and reuse everywhere.  
3. Add one annotation accessor layer so rendering/queue logic reads from a single helper instead of open-coding override lookups.

What should *not* be done if behavior must stay identical: deleting these features or collapsing skip kinds to temporary-only.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/promoted_ids.py\n+\"\"\"Helpers for tracking user-promoted queue IDs.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+\n+def _promoted_ids(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n+    promoted = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\")\n+    if isinstance(promoted, list):\n+        return promoted\n+    promoted = []\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+    return promoted\n+\n+\n+def add_promoted_ids(plan: dict[str, Any], issue_ids: list[str]) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Append issue IDs to promoted_ids preserving existing order.\"\"\"\n+    promoted = _promoted_ids(plan)\n+    existing = set(promoted)\n+    for issue_id in issue_ids:\n+        if issue_id in existing:\n+            continue\n+        promoted.append(issue_id)\n+        existing.add(issue_id)\n+\n+\n+def prune_promoted_ids(plan: dict[str, Any], issue_ids: set[str] | list[str]) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from promoted_ids if present.\"\"\"\n+    remove_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    if not remove_set:\n+        return\n+    promoted = _promoted_ids(plan)\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [issue_id for issue_id in promoted if issue_id not in remove_set]\n+\n+\n+def promoted_insertion_index(order: list[str], plan: dict[str, Any]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Return insertion index immediately after the last promoted item.\"\"\"\n+    promoted = set(_promoted_ids(plan))\n+    if not promoted:\n+        return 0\n+    last_idx = -1\n+    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if issue_id in promoted:\n+            last_idx = idx\n+    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"add_promoted_ids\",\n+    \"promoted_insertion_index\",\n+    \"prune_promoted_ids\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/annotations.py\n+\"\"\"Read helpers for per-issue plan override annotations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+\n+def get_issue_override(plan: dict[str, Any], issue_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    \"\"\"Return override payload for an issue ID or an empty mapping.\"\"\"\n+    overrides = plan.get(\"overrides\", {})\n+    if not isinstance(overrides, dict):\n+        return {}\n+    payload = overrides.get(issue_id, {})\n+    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n+        return payload\n+    return {}\n+\n+\n+def get_issue_description(plan: dict[str, Any], issue_id: str) -> str | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return per-issue plan description if one is set.\"\"\"\n+    value = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id).get(\"description\")\n+    return value if isinstance(value, str) else None\n+\n+\n+def get_issue_note(plan: dict[str, Any], issue_id: str) -> str | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return per-issue plan note if one is set.\"\"\"\n+    value = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id).get(\"note\")\n+    return value if isinstance(value, str) else None\n+\n+\n+def annotation_counts(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:\n+    \"\"\"Return counts of non-empty descriptions and notes in overrides.\"\"\"\n+    overrides = plan.get(\"overrides\", {})\n+    if not isinstance(overrides, dict):\n+        return 0, 0\n+    described = 0\n+    noted = 0\n+    for payload in overrides.values():\n+        if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n+            continue\n+        if payload.get(\"description\"):\n+            described += 1\n+        if payload.get(\"note\"):\n+            noted += 1\n+    return described, noted\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"annotation_counts\",\n+    \"get_issue_description\",\n+    \"get_issue_note\",\n+    \"get_issue_override\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  - [stale_dimensions.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py)

> AGENT

Done. I fixed the broken parts without re-adding backward-compat shims.

**What I changed**
1. Fixed real runtime behavior in Bandit adapter:
- [bandit_adapter.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/detectors/bandit_adapter.py)
  - Zone filtering now excludes `TEST` and `CONFIG` (in addition to `GENERATED`/`VENDOR`).
  - Zone lookup now uses normalized relative path.
  - Bandit command now uses an absolute scan path (`path.resolve()`).

2. Updated tests to current APIs (no compat globals/functions restored):
- [test_cmd_scan.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/scan/test_cmd_scan.py)
  - `emit_scorecard_badge` mock now returns a tuple, matching real contract.
- [test_cmd_resolve.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_resolve.py)
  - Patched current hook (`_write_resolve_query_entry`) instead of removed `write_query`.
  - Updated suppress test to patch `_save_config_or_exit` in the suppress module.
- [test_skip.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_skip.py)
  - Log-cap monkeypatch now targets `operations_meta._get_log_cap` (current location).
- TypeScript detector tests now use runtime-root fixture instead of removed `PROJECT_ROOT` module attrs:
  - [test_ts_concerns.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_concerns.py)
  - [test_ts_props.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_props.py)
  - [test_ts_react.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_react.py)
  - [test_ts_smells.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_smells.py)
  - [test_ts_unused.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/tests/test_ts_unused.py)

- Targeted previously failing groups: all green.
